Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the hydrocarbons framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 262. The resulting operator achieves bound O(383/n^3).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the hydrocarbons space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 262 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 383. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any hydrocarbons module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 642. The projective resolution has length at most 188 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on hydrocarbons structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 642. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 188. QED.
